	}
	text = {
		trigger = { always = yes }
		localization_key = DD_ISR_SLOT_EMPTY
	}
}

defined_text = {
	name = GetDdIsrDiasporaStatus
	text = {
		trigger = { check_variable = { dd_isr_diaspora_pick_slot > 0 } }
		localization_key = DD_ISR_DIASPORA_STATUS_PICK
	}
	text = {
		trigger = { check_variable = { dd_isr_diaspora_selected_slot > 0 } }
		localization_key = DD_ISR_DIASPORA_STATUS_SELECTED
	}
	text = {
		trigger = { always = yes }
		localization_key = DD_ISR_DIASPORA_STATUS_IDLE
	}
}
